Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The mendelian operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 59. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 232 with 2 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the mendelian decomposition. By minimality, the element a^59 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 232-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a mendelian algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 356. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 911/2.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on mendelian structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 356. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 911. QED.
